uyiriṉum ciṟappap peṟṟōrāl talaimakaḷ pēṇappaṭum iyalpiṉaik kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻuki aṟintuḷāṉākaliṉ, kātal maṭamakaḷ eṉṟum, poruṭkoṭai etirntu ataṟku nēreṉa makaṭkoṭai yāṟṟum peṟṟōr māṇpaṟiyātu vēṇṭap paṭāta tuyaram nalki, vēṇṭi niṟkum uṟakkattai valintu kavarntamaiyiṉ, maṭamakaḷ eṉṟum, taṉṉiṟ ṟīrntu cēymaiyiṟ pirintuḻiyum taṉ pirivāṉākiya tuyaram, neñcil niṉṟu varuttu taliṉ, keṭalarun tuyaram eṉṟum, itukāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyātu, ivaḷoṭu toṭarpu peṟṟa piṉpē kāṇutaliṉ, nalki eṉṟum, tuyaruṟṟārkku uṟakkamiṉmai iyalpākaliṉ, paṭaliṉ pāyal vauviyōḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. uṟakkattiṉkaṇ tām pirinta kātalaraik kaṉaviṭaik kāṇum kāṭciyiṉpa muṭaimai yālum, tuyarāṟ kalakkuṟṟa neñcam tuyiliṟkup piṉ teḷintu mēl ceyyakkaṭavaṉavaṟṟait teḷitalālum, paṭaliṉ pāyal eṉṟum, tāṉ aṟiyāvakaiyil nīṅkiṉamai yiṉ, vauvi yōḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
vaḷaiyīṉṟa muttiṉait tantu paratavar vēṇṭuva koḷvar eṉṟatu, ivaḷ emakkut tuyaram nalkip pāyalaik koṇṭāḷ eṉṟaṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu. vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuppak koṇṭu tām aritiṟ peṟṟataṉaik koṭukkum peṟṟiyar avaḷ tamarāka, avaḷ yām vēṇṭātatu koṭuttu vēṇṭiṟṟuk koṇṭāḷ eṉpatoru nayam tōṉṟutal kāṇka. vēṇṭātatu keṭalarun tuyaram; vēṇṭiṟṟu, paṭaliṉ pāyal. itaṉāl, talaimakaṉ kaṇ kaṇṭuyil maṟuttal eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. iḵtu oruvakaiyāl, peṟṟōraip paḻicciyatumām.
vaḷaipaṭu muttam paratavar pakarum kaṭalkeḻu koṇ kaṉ eṉṟataṉāl talaimakaḷaip peṟal eḷimaiyum, keṭalarum tuyaram nalkip paṭaliṉ pāyal vauvi yōḷē eṉṟa taṉāl pirivarumaiyum tōṉṟak kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, peṟaleḷimai poruḷvayiṉ uḷḷattait turappa, pirivarumai mīḷaluṟutta, iṭaiyē ceyviṉai viraiya muṭiyāmai varuttat talaimakaṉ āṟṟātu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉāṉ eṉa uṇarka. “puṟantāḻ piruṇṭa kūntaṟ pōtiṉ, niṟampeṟum īritaḻp polinta uṇkaṇ, uḷḷam piṇikkoṇ ṭōḷvayiṉ neñcam, cellal tīrkañ celvā meṉṉum, ceyviṉai muṭiyā tevvam ceytal, eyyā maiyō ṭiḷivutalait tarumeṉa, uṟuti tūkkat tūṅki aṟivē, ciṟunaṉi viraiyal eṉṉum āyiṭai, oḷiṟēntu maruppiṟ kaḷiṟu māṟupaṟṟiya, tēypurip paḻaṅkayiṟupōla, vīvatu kolleṉ varuntiya uṭampē 1” eṉṟu talaimakaṉ niṉaittu varuntumāṟu kāṇka.
“marapunilai tiriyā māṭciya vāki, viravum poruḷum viravu meṉpa 2” eṉpuḻi, “varaiviṭai vaittup pirintōṉ talaiviyai niṉaintu varuntik kūṟiyatu” eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.
iṉip paṭaliṉpāyal vaṟuvi yōḷē eṉṟa pāṭattukku, pāyal illai yākkiṉāḷ eṉpatu poruḷāyiṉum, mēṟ kāṭṭiya nayam tōṉṟaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟātal kāṇka.
	196.	kōṭī relvaḷaik koḻumpal kūntal
āytoṭi maṭavaral vēṇṭuti yāyiṉ
teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāp paṭūum
taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa varaintaṉai koṇmō.
itu, kuṟaimaṟukkappaṭṭa talaimakaṉ piṉṉum kuṟai vēṇṭiya vaḻit tōḻi colliyatu.
paḻaiya urai :
teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāp paṭūum cērppa eṉṟatu, niṉaittaṉa akappaṭuttum tuṟaivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
urai :
caṅkiṉai aṟuttuc ceyta vaḷaiyiṉaiyum, koḻuviya palavākiya kūntaliṉaiyum, nuṇṇiya toṭiyiṉaiyu muṭaiya maṭavaralākiya ivaḷaik kūṭutal vēṇṭiṉai yāyiṉ, teḷinta nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kaḻikkaṇ vēṇṭuvōr koḷḷu māṟu cevviya iṟāmīṉkaḷ akappaṭum kuḷirnta kaṭaṉilat talaivaṉē, vēṟu vakai yiṉmaiyiṉ varaintu kōṭalaic ceyvāyāka.
vaḷai, caṅkiṉai aṟuttuc ceyyappaṭum eṉpataṉai, “vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaram tumitta vaḷaikaḷain toḻinta koḻuntu 1” eṉṟu kūṟumāṟṟāṉ aṟika. “kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷai nekiḻtta, pīṭukeḻu kuricil 2” eṉṟār piṟarum. iruṇṭu ceṟintiruttaliṉ, koḻum pal kūntal eṉappaṭṭatu. toṭi, vaḷai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṉṉaṇivatu. vaḷai caṅkiṉālāyatu; toṭi, vaḷaiyālum maruppālu māyatu eṉa uṇarka. vaḷaiyum, kūntalum,  toṭiyum uṭaiya maṭavaral eṉa iyaiyum. vaḷaiyum toṭiyum kūntalum eṉṉātu, vaḷaiyum kūntalum cērak kūṟiyatu, cilavum palavumām iyaipu karuti yeṉa aṟika. “cilvaḷai viṟali 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. ovvoru kaikkum ovvoru toṭiyē aṇiyappaṭu mākaliṉ, ataṉaip pirittuk kūṟiṉār.
orukāl kuṟai maṟukkappaṭṭavaṉ, varaintukōṭalait teruḷātu, piṉṉum kuṟaiyirantu niṟṟaliṉ, maṭavaral vēṇṭutiyāyiṉ eṉṟum, kuṟaiyuṟṟuk kūṭutal peṟiṉum atu piṉṉarp pirivukkum, alarkkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum añcappaṭṭuk kūṭāmaiyē pōl varuttam ceyyumākaliṉ, varaintaṉai koṇmō eṉṟum, uḷḷuṟaiyāl varaitaṟkuriya muṟaimai yum kūṟiṉāḷ.
teṇkaḻiyiṭattu vēṇṭuvōr koḷḷumāṟu iṟāmīṉ kaḷ akappaṭum tuṟaiva eṉṟataṉāl, nī vēṇṭivariṉ tamar makaṭkoṭai nērva rātaliṉ varaintu koḷka eṉṟāḷām.
koḻumaṇik kūntal eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, koḻuviya maṇi pōlum kariya kūntal eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. “maṇiyēcum iruṅkūntal 2” eṉavum, “maṇimaru ḷaimpāl 3” eṉavum cāṉ ṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 4” eṉṟa cūttirattu “ulakurait toḻippiṉum” eṉpataṟku iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārkkiṉi yarum itaṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭuvar.
	197.	ilaṅkuvaḷai teḷirppa valava ṉāṭṭi
mukamputai katuppiṉa ḷiṟaiñciniṉ ṟōḷē
pulampukoṇ mālai maṟaiya
nalaṅkē ḻāka nalkuva ḷeṉakkē.
itu, talaimakaṉ iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talaiviyatu nilaimai kaṇṭu colliyatu.
urai :
viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaikaḷ olikka alavaṉaik kālāl alaittu, mukattai maṟaikkum kūntalaiyuṭaiyaḷāyt talai kuṉintu niṉṟavaḷ taṉimai koḷḷum mālaip poḻutu maṟaitalum, naṉmai poruntiya taṉ mārpiṉai eṉakku nalkuvaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
teḷirttal - olittal. alavaṉ - naṇṭu. āṭṭutal - alaittal. katuppiṉa ḷeṉṉum muṟṟeccam iṟaiñci niṉ ṟōḷ eṉṉum viṉaippeyar koṇṭatu. taṉimaiyuṟṟārkku ēṟṟa kālamākaliṉ, pulampukoḷ mālai eṉappaṭṭatu. ceyaveṉeccam, itu nikaḻāniṟka itu nikaḻum eṉum uṭa ṉikaḻccip poruṭṭu.
alava ṉāṭṭutal makaḷir viḷaiyāṭṭu vakai. “tiṇi maṇal aṭaikarai alavaṉ āṭṭi, acaiyiṉaḷ irunta āytoṭik kuṟumakaḷ 1” eṉṟum, “perunīr aḻuvat tentai tanta, koḻumī ṉuṇaṅkaṟ paṭupuḷ ōppi, ekkarp puṉṉaiyiṉ iṉṉiḻal acaiic, cekkar ñeṇṭiṉ kuṇṭaḷai keṇṭi, ñāḻal ōṅkuciṉait toṭutta koṭuṅkaḻit, tāḻai vīḻkayiṟṟu ūcal tūṅkik koṇṭa liṭumaṇaṟ kuravai muṉaiyiṉ, veṇṭalaip puṇari yāyamo ṭāṭi, maṇippūm paintaḻai taii aṇittakap, palpūṅ kāṉal alkiṉam varutal 2” eṉṟum varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟika. alavaṉai alaikkuṅkāl, uṭal kuluṅkak kaiyum acaitaliṉ vaḷai olippatu paṟṟi ilaṅku vaḷai teḷirppa eṉṟār. “cilavilaṅ kelvaḷai ñekiḻa, alavaṉ āṭṭuvōḷ 3” eṉṟār piṟarum. aḷaiyiṭai yamarntu alavaṉaik kaiyāl alaittalum  uṇmaiyiṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟalum oṉṟu.
cēymaikkaṇ varumiṭattu avaḷatu uṇmai uṇarttu taliṉ, ilaṅkuvaḷai teḷirppa eṉṟum, avaḷ taṉṉai niṉaintu niṟkum niṉaiviṉai, nōkkuvārkku mukam uṇart tāmaipporuṭṭuk kūntal nekiḻntu mukam putaiyac carintu kiṭantamaiyiṉ, mukamputai katuppiṉaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. uḷḷa nikaḻcciyai mukam muṉṉam uṇarttum mutukkuṟai vuṭaiya teṉpatu, “muṉṉam mukam pōla muṉṉuraippatil 4” eṉpataṉāl aṟika. iyaṟkaiyē nāṇmika vuṭaiyaḷ ākaliṉ, iṟaiñci niṉṟōḷ eṉṟāṉ. “nāṇaṭac cāynta nalaṅkiḷar eruttiṉ 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. mālai maṟaitalum tamittuk kūṭaluṟuvārkkuk kūṭṭam iṭaiyīṭiṉṟi iṉitu eytu mākaliṉ, pulampukoḷ mālai maṟaiya eṉṟum, iyaṟkaip puṇarccikkaṇ tāṉ talaippeyta viṭattu, ivvaṇṇamē etirppaṭṭā ḷākaliṉ, avviyaipiṉāl iṉik kūṭṭa meytutal orutalai yeṉat teḷintu kūṟukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, nalaṅkē ḻākam nalkuvaḷ eṉakkē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
iṉi, “ilaṅkuvaḷai teḷirppa alava ṉāṭṭi”yatu, pūkkoytal mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭiṉaic ceyyāmai kuṟittu āyattār ārāyāmaip poruṭ ṭeṉṟum, puṟattē alavaṉai alaittu niṉṟā ḷāyiṉum, akattē taṉṉai niṉaintu niṉṟamaiyiṉ, iṟaiñci niṉṟāḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
katuppiṉaḷ āyattu niṉṟōḷē eṉṟa pāṭattukkuk katuppiṉaḷāy āyattiṭai niṉṟāḷāyiṉum, mālai maṟai talum ākam nalkuvaḷ eṉa uraikka. āyiṉum, itu poruḷ ciṟavāmai uṇarka.
neytaṟkaṇ puṇartal kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, itu tiṇai mayakkam. “tiṇaimayak kuṟutalum kaṭinilai yilavē 2” eṉpatu viti. iṉi, “meytoṭṭup payiṟal 3” eṉṟa cūttirattuk kūṭuta luṟutal taṉiyē vantataṟku itaṉaik kāṭṭuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talaimakkaḷ tāmē kūṭuta luṇṭēṉum, perunāṇiṉaḷāya talaimakaḷpāl talaimakaṉ, meytoṭṭup payiṟal mutalāyiṉa viṉṟik kūṭutaluṟutal amaiyāmaiyālum, avai ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟku itaṉ kaṇ coṟ kuṟippu oṉṟēṉum iṉmaiyālum, tiṇaimayakkat tukku utāraṇam kāṭṭiyavaḻi “iḵtu iṭantalaip pāṭṭiṉ kaṇ talaiviyatu nilai kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu” eṉṟē kūṟi irutta lālum atu karuttaṉmai yaṟika.
	198.	vaḷaiyaṇi muṉkai vāleyiṟ ṟamarnakai
iḷaiya rāṭum taḷaiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ
kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi niṉṟa
neṭuntō ḷaṇṇaṟ kaṇṭikum yāmē.
itu, parattaiyar maṉaikkaṭ paṉṉāḷ taṅkip piṉpu āṟṟāmaiyē vāyilāka vanta talaimakaṉai etirppaṭṭa tōḻi talaimakaṭkuc colliyatu.
paḻaiya urai :
kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟu kaḷavukkālattu nikaḻntataṉaik kūṟiṟṟu, talaimakaḷ pulavi nīṅku taṟkum tāṉ neṭunāṭ pirintamai kūṟukiṉṟavāṟu eṉṟu avaṉ varuntutaṟkum eṉak koḷka.
urai :
vaḷai yaṇinta muṉkaiyaiyum, veḷḷiya paṟkaḷ viḷaṅkum muṟuvalaiyu muṭaiya iḷamakaḷi rāṭum muṟukka viḻum malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṉaliṭattuk kuṟuntuṟai  yāṇṭuḷatu eṉa viṉavi niṉṟa neṭiya tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya aṇṇalai yām kāṇpōm, varuka eṉṟavāṟu.
amarnakai - viruppan tarum nakai eṉṟumām. iḷaiya rāṭum kuṟuntuṟai, taḷaiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ kuṟuntuṟai yeṉa iyaiyum. iṭattu nikaḻ poruṭkuriya aṭai iṭattiṉmēl niṉṟatu. iḷaiyarāṭum tuṟaiyātal paṟṟik kuṟuntuṟai eṉappaṭṭatu. viṉavi niṉṟa aṇṇal eṉka.
parattaiyar maṉaikkaṇ paṉṉāḷ taṅki avar kūṭṭamē niṉaintu oḻukiṉāṉ eṉpataṉaik kuṟippāl, vaḷaiyaṇi muṉkai vāleyiṟṟu amarnakai iḷaiyarāṭum taḷai yaviḻ kāṉal kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi niṉṟa eṉṟum, atu kāraṇamākat talaimakaḷ pulakkumeṉṟu añci, kaḷaviṉkaṇ yām puṉaliṭai vīḻntu keṭap puka, kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi niṉṟu nammai yeṭutta uyarnta tōḷai eṇṇip pulavātoḻital vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum kūṟi ṉāḷ. nampāl nikaḻnta melivu kaṇṭu vārātu, taṉ āṟ ṟāmaiyē vāyilākap pukuntāṉai nērtalai nām ceyaṟpāṟṟu eṉṟaṟkuk, kaṇṭikum yāmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “iḷaiya rāṭum taḷaiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi” niṉṟatu, kaḷaviṉ kaṇ matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟku; “ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum, nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit tōḻiyaik 1” kuṟaiyuṟuvāṉ kūṟiyataṉai niṉaintu kūṟiya teṉka. iḵtu aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉ pirivāṟṟāmai.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 2” eṉṟa cūttirattu, “kuṟainta vaṭ paṭariṉum eṉṟatu, mēl talaivaṉatu puṇarcciyuṇmai yaṟintu tāḻntu niṉṟa tōḻi, tāṉum kuṟaiyuṟṟut talaivi māṭṭuc cellutaṟkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum” eṉṟum, “ik kiḷavi irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talaivaṉ uḷḷappuṇarcci uḷvaḻiyum, kuṟaiyuṟṟu meyyuṟu puṇarcci vēṇṭit talaivimāṭṭuc cellum kālattum okkum” eṉṟum kūṟi itaṉai utāraṇam kāṭṭuvar iḷampūraṇar; ivvāṟē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum 2 koḷvar. itaṟkum poruḷ okkumāṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, vaḷaiyaṟai muṉkai eṉṟa pāṭattukku vaḷai olikkum muṉkai eṉṟu uraikka. vāleyiṟ ṟiṉṉakai eṉṟum, virunteṉa viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum, neṭuntēr aṇṇal eṉṟum varum pāṭaṅkaṭku muṟaiyē, vāliya eyiṟu viḷaṅkum iṉiya muṟuval eṉṟum, “putiyēṉ mika, ippuṉattiṟku yāṉ taṉip pōntaṉaṉ” eṉa ūrum pērum neṟiyum viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum, tērūrntu varutalum kaḷaviṉkaṇ urittākaliṉ, neṭiya tērai yuṭaiya aṇṇal eṉṟum poruḷ uraittukkoḷka. tuṟai iraṇṭaṟkum poruḷ okkum eṉka.
	199.	kāṉalam peruntuṟaik kalitirai tiḷaikkum
vāṉuyar neṭumaṇa lēṟi yāṉātu
kāṇkam vammō tōḻi
ceṟivaḷai nekiḻttō ṉeṟikaṭa ṉāṭē.
itu, talaimakaṉ oruvaḻit taṇantuḻi āṟṟāḷākiya talai makaḷai āṟṟuvikkum tōḻi colliyatu.
urai :
tōḻi, nam muṉkaikkaṇ ceṟintu niṉṟa vaḷaikaḷ nekiḻntōṭuvitta talaimakaṉatu alai yeṟiyum kaṭal nāṭṭiṉaik kāṉaṟ peruntuṟaikkaṇ muḻaṅkukiṉṟa alaikaḷ pōntu alaikkum vāṉuṟa uyarnta neṭiya maṇaṟṟiṭarmēl ēṟi āṇṭē amaintoḻiyātu avaṉatu varaviṉaik kāṇpām, varuka eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṉalam peruntuṟai eṉpuḻi ammuccāriyai virikkum vaḻi virittal. vāṉuyar neṭumaṇal, mika uyarnta maṇaṟkuṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu. neytal nilat talaimaka ṉākaliṉ nekiḻttōṉ eṟikaṭal nāṭu eṉṟār.
kuṟivaḻip piḻaiyātu pōntu nalaṉ nukarntu ceṉṟavaṉ taṇattalāl mēṉi melintu vaḷai nekiḻa niṉṟamaiyiṉ, ceṟivaḷai nekiḻttōṉ eṉṟum, avaṉatu nāṭu kāṇṭalum avaṉaik kaṇṭāṟ pōlum iṉpam payakku mākaliṉ eṟi kaṭal nāṭu eṉṟum, neṭu maṇaṟ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟavaḻi āṇṭut tōṉṟum iyaṟkaikkāṭci neñciṉkaṇ nilavum varuttam keṭat taṉ iyaṟkaiyiṉpam payantu neñcaip piṇikku mākaliṉ vāṉuyar neṭumaṇal ēṟi āṉātu eṉṟum, uyariya maṇaṟ kuṉṟa mākaliṉ avaṉatu kaṭalnāṭṭiṉai muṭiyak kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉpāḷ, kāṇkam vammō eṉṟum kūṟi ṉāḷ. “kuṉṟiṉ tōṉṟum kuvavumaṇa lēṟik, kaṇṭaṉam varukam ceṉmō 1” eṉṟār piṟarum, “orutalai yurimai vēṇṭiyum 2” eṉṟaviṭattu, “oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi mēṉa” eṉṟataṉāl, tōḻimāṭṭup pōkkum varavum tōṉṟum eṉak koḷka. ceṟivaḷai nekiḻttōṉ eṉṟatu uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 3” eṉṟa cūttirattu, vēṇ ṭāp piriviṉkaṇ nikaḻum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ itu talaiviyai āṟṟuvittatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
neṭuvarai yēṟi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉum, neṭiya malaimē lēṟi avaṉatu eṟikaṭal nāṭṭiṉaik kāṇkam vammō eṉa uraikka. neytal nilattē varaikaḷ uḷavāyiṉum avai neyta lākumēyaṉṟik kuṟiñci eṉak koḷḷaṟka; “nila ṉoruṅku mayaṅkutal iṉṟu eṉa moḻipa 1” eṉpatu poru ḷilakkaṇam.
	200.	ilaṅkuvīṅ kelvaḷai yāynutal kaviṉap
polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉa ṉiṉiyē 
vilaṅkari neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati
nalaṅkavar pacalaiyai nakukam yāmē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇintavaḻi ataṟku iraviṉkaṇ talaimakaṉ vantatu aṟinta tōḻi talaimakaḷaip pāyal uṇarttic colliyatu.
urai :
viḷakkamuṟa nekiḻntu ilaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaiyiṉaiyuṭai yāy, niṉ oḷi nuṇukiya neṟṟi aḻakeytumāṟu poṟṟēri ṉaiyuṭaiya koṇkaṉ ippoḻutu uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟaṟku vantuḷā ṉākaliṉ, vilaṅkutalaiyuṭaiya ari paranta neṭiya kaṇkaḷ tuyil nīṅkit teḷivāyāka; ataṉāl nam nalam kavarnta pacalaiyiṉ kēṭu kaṇṭu yām makiḻalām eṉṟavāṟu.
vīṅku eṉṉum mutaṉilait taṉiviṉai viṉaiyeccap poruṭṭu; “varippuṉai pantu 2” eṉṟāṟ pōla. āynutal eṉpuḻi āytal curuṅkutal; pacalaiyāl oḷi curuṅkiṉamaiyiṉ āynutal eṉappaṭṭatu. iṉi, elvaḷaiyāy eṉavum, nutal kaviṉ eṉavum koḷḷiṉum amaiyum. kaviṉa eṉṉum peyaraṭiyākap piṟanta viṉaiyeccam vantaṉaṉ eṉṉum kāraṇaviṉai koṇṭatu. poṉ eṉpatu, ceyyuḷākaliṉ, polam eṉa vantatu.3 ñekiḻtal īṇṭut tuyileḻutal. nakukam: paṉmait taṉmai. nakai, eḷḷal paṟṟip piṟappatu. kaṇṇiṉ veḷviḻiyil mēlum kīḻumākac civanta nuṇṇiya koṭipōl parantirukkum varikaḷai vilaṅkari eṉpa; neṭu viḻiyiṉ kuṟukkē niṟṟaliṉ vilaṅkari eṉak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
varaivu nīṭṭittalāl, iṟceṟippu mutaliyavaṟṟāl mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭu nutal pacantu vāṭi yiruntā ḷākaliṉ, āynutal eṉṟum, aḻivil kūṭṭattā ṉaṉṟi appacappu nīṅkāmai uṭkoṇṭu uṭaṉpōkku niṉaintiruntāḷākaliṉ āy nutal kaviṉa eṉṟum, pōkkiṉkaṇ tamar etirntu takaiyāvāṟu viraipari pūṭṭiya tēroṭu talaimakaṉ vantuḷāṉ eṉṟaṟkup polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ ip poḻutē vantāṉāyiṉum nī tāḻttal kūṭā teṉṟaṟku iṉiyē vilaṅkari neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati eṉṟum, talai makaṉ pirivālum varaivu nīṭṭittamaiyālum nutal oḷi karappap paṭarntu māmaikkaviṉ keṭa iruntamaiyiṉ nalaṅ kavar pacalai eṉṟum, talaivaṉoṭu uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi eytum aḻivil kūṭṭattāl pacalai paṭartal illai yeṉpāḷ nakukam yām eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭa talaimakaṉ iraviṉkaṇ vantamai aṟintatum, tōḻi talaimakaḷait tuyiluṇarttuvāḷ avaḷ virainteḻiṉ vaḷaiyoliyāl tāyar mutalāyiṉār kaṇ viḻippa reṉpatu aṟivittaṟku elvaḷai eṉavum, avvāṟu eḻuvāṭkut talaimakaṉ pōkku niṉaintu vantamai uṇart taṟkup polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉaṉ eṉavum, neṟipaṟṟit talaimakaṉpāl viraintu aṭaiya vēṇṭutaliṉ “eḻuka” eṉṉātu neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati eṉavum, pacalai parantamai nōkki alar kūṟum ayaṟpeṇṭir talaimaṭaṅku vataṉaik kuṟippumoḻiyāl nalaṅkavar pacalaiyai nakukam yāmē eṉavum tōḻi cila coṟkaḷāl viḷaṅka vuṇarttum tiṟam kāṇka.
nutal kaviṉpeṟa eṉṟum, neṭuntērk koṇkaṉum eṉṟum, neṭuṅkaṇ aṉantal tīrmati eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. avaṟṟiṟku muṟaiyē nutal tāṉ iḻanta aḻakaip peṟa eṉṟum, neṭiya tēraiyuṭaiya talaimakaṉum eṉṟum, ummaiyāl avaṉ pōkku uṭaṉpaṭātiruntamaiyum, kaṇkaḷāṟ peṟum tuyil nīṅkuka eṉṟum uraikka. nalaṅkiḷar pacalai eṉṟa pāṭamum kuṟippumoḻiyāy nalaṅ kavarnta pacalai eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum eṉka.
āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar neṭuṅkaṇ aṉantar tīrmati eṉṟē pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, “talaivarum viḻuma nilai yeṭut turaippiṉum 1” eṉpuḻip “pōkkaṟ kaṇṇum” eṉṟataṟku itaṉaik kāṭṭi, “kaṇkaḷ aṉantal tīrmati eṉṟata ṉālē uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōvataṟku vantāṉ eṉap pāya luṇarttik kūṟiṟṟu” eṉṟār. itu neytaṟkaṇ pālai vantatu.
itukāṟum urai kūṟip pōnta neytaṟ pāṭṭukkaḷ nūṟum, tāykkuraitta pattu mutal vaḷaippattu īṟākap pattup pattukka ḷāka vakukkap peṟṟiruppatu kaṇṭōm. ivaṟṟuḷ tāyk kuraitta pattu, tōḻik kuraitta pattu, kiḻavaṟkuraitta pattu, pāṇaṟ kuraitta pattu eṉṟa nāṉkum uripporuḷaic cārnta kēṭpōr  poruḷākavum, ñāḻaṟpattu, veḷḷāṅ kurukup pattu, ciṟuveṇ kākkaippattu, toṇṭippattu, neytaṟ pattu, vaḷaippattu eṉṟa āṟum neytaṟ karupporuḷ poru ḷākavum tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mutaliṟ karuvum, karuviṉ uriyum “muṟai ciṟantaṉa 2” eṉpataṉāl uripporuṭ kuriya tāyar mutaliyōrai muṟpaṭa niṟuttiyum, ēṉaik karup poruḷaip piṟpaṭa niṟuttiyum inneytaṟ pakutiyaip pulat
tuṟai muṟṟiya kūṭalūr kiḻār tokuttuḷḷār.
iraṇṭāvatu
āciriyar ammūvaṉār pāṭiya neytal muṟṟum.

4.	parattaiyar, kātaṟparattai, iṟparattai, cēripparattai eṉap palavakaiyāyk kūṟappaṭuvar. avarait tollāciriyar parattaiyar eṉap potuppaṭa moḻivar.
	iṉi, kāmakkiḻattiyar, parattaiyar eṉa vēṟupaṭuttu, “kāmakkiḻattiya rāvār piṉmuṟai yākkiya kiḻattiyar; avar mūvakaippaṭuvar: otta kiḻattiyarum, iḻinta kiḻattiyarum. varaiyappaṭṭārum eṉa. otta kiḻattiyar, muntuṟṟa maṉaiyāḷaṉṟik kāmam poruḷākap piṉṉum taṉ kulattuḷḷāḷ oruttiyai varaital; iḻintavarāvār
	antaṇarkku araca kulattiṉum, vaṇika kulattiṉum, vēḷāṇ kulattiṉum koṭukkappaṭṭārum, aracarkku ēṉaiyiraṇṭu kulattiṉum koṭukkappaṭṭārum, vaṇikarkku vēḷāṇ kulattiṟ koṭukkappaṭṭārum. varaiyappaṭṭār, celvarāyiṉār kaṇikaik kulattiṉuḷḷārkku iṟkiḻamai koṭuttu varaintu koḷḷappaṭṭār. avar, kaṉṉiyil varaiyappaṭṭārum, ataṉ piṉpu varaiyappaṭṭārum eṉa iruvakaiyar. avviruvarum urimai pūṇṭamaiyāṟ kāmakkiḻattiyarpāṟ paṭṭaṉar. parattaiyarāvar yāreṉiṉ, avar āṭalum pāṭalum vallarāki aḻakum iḷamaiyuṅkāṭṭi iṉpamum poruḷum veḵki oruvar māṭṭum taṅkātār” eṉpar āciriyar iḷampūraṇar (tol. 149 : urai.)
	nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, kāmakkiḻattiyar, parattaiyar eṉpāruḷ. “kāmakkiḻattiyarāvār kaṭaṉaṟiyum vāḻkkai yuṭaiyarākik kāmak kiḻamaipūṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum parattaiyar” eṉṟum, “avar talaivaṉatu iḷamaipparuvattiṟ kūṭi mutirntōrum, avaṉ talainiṉṟu oḻukappaṭum iḷamaip paruvattōrum, iṭainilaip paruvat
tōrum, kāmañcālā iḷamaiyōrum eṉap pala pakutiyiṉarām” eṉṟum, ēṉaip parattaiyar, “kūttum pāṭṭum uṭaiyarāki varum cēripparattaiyarum, kulattiṉkaṇ iḻintōrum, aṭiyarum, viṉaivala pāṅkiṉarum piṟarumām” eṉṟum kūṟi, iḷampūraṇar ottakiḻattiyar mutal mūvakaiyiṉarākak kūṟiya pākupāṭṭaic ciṟappuṭait talaiviyarōṭu parattaiyaraiyuṅ kūṭṭik kāmakkiḻattiya reṉṟu cūttirañ ceyyiṉ mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟam taṅkumākaliṉ, aḵtu āciriyarkkuk karuttaṉṟu eṉa maṟuttu, “cāṉṟōr palarum kāmakkiḻattiyaraip parattaiyarākat tōṟṟuvāy ceytu kūṟumāṟum uṇarka” (tol.po. 151.) eṉpar.
	nāṟkavirāca nampiyār, “oruvaṉ taṉakkē yurimai pūṇṭu. varukulap parattaiyar makaḷirākik, kāmakku varaintār kāmak kiḻattiyar, “yāraiyum nayavā viyalpiṟ ciṟanta, cērip parattaiyar makaḷi rākik, kātaliṟ puṇarntōr kātaṟ parattaiyar”, “avaruḷum varaitaṟ kuriyā ruḷarē” (113-5) eṉpar-iḵtu uraikaḷil varumāṟu. nāṭṭil aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrkaḷāl, makaḷiriṉum āṭavar tokai kuṟaintatāka, āṭavariṉṟi mikka makaḷir parattaiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. parattaiyar, mikkavar. itu varalāṟu kāṭṭumāṟu.

1.	paṇṭai nāḷil tēṉūr eṉṟa peyaruṭaiya ūrkaḷ pala iruntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nilaipēṟuṭaiya kuṟippukkaḷ koṇṭavai pāṇṭināṭṭil iraṇṭum, naṭunāṭṭil oṉṟum kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. naṭunāṭṭiṉuḷḷatu peṇṇait teṉkarai makatai nāṭṭut teṉkaraip paḻaṅkūrppaṟṟut tēṉūr (A.R. 409 of 1938) eṉappaṭukiṟatu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil mutukuḷattūrp pakutiyil tirumālukantāṉ kōṭṭaiyeṉpatu paṇṭai nāḷil tēṉūr eṉṟa peyarkoṇṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntatu. itaṉaik kalveṭṭukaḷ, tirumāvukantanallūr eṉṟum, ēḻurc cempi nāṭṭut teṉmuṉaippaṟṟut tēṉūrāṉa tirumāvukanta nallūr (A.R. 43 of 1931-32) eṉṟum, aḷaṟṟunāṭṭut tēṉūr (A.R. 56 of 1931-32) eṉṟum, kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. tēṉūraic cārnta ēḻūr tirunāvukkaracarāl (284.5) ciṟappikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. maṟṟoṉṟu vaiyaikkaraiyil tiruvēṭakattuk kaṇmaiyil uḷatu. tiruvēṭakam ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil tiruñāṉa campantar paravum ciṟappup peṟṟatu. ataṉai aṭuttirukkum tēṉūraip paḻaṅkālak kalveṭṭukkaḷ pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattup peruntēṉūr (A.R. 604 of 1926) eṉavum, pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattuk
	 tēṉūrt tiruvēṭakam eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta kalveṭṭukkaḷ, itaṉait tēṉūrāṉa virutarāca payaṅkara nallūr (606 of 1926) eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ittēṉūr vaiyai pāyum nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum mikuntu iṉṟum ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvatōṭu “vēṉilāyiṉum taṇpuṉaloḻukum” ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmalē yuḷatu, itaṉ veṟṟilai vaḷattai, tēṉūr veṟṟilaiyum, māṉūr cuṇṇāmpum eṉṟa paḻamoḻi kāṭṭi niṟkiṟatu. tiruñāṉacampantar “vāṉūrāṉ vaiyakattāṉ vāḻttuvār maṉattakattāṉ. tēṉūrāṉ ceṅkāṭṭaṅ kuṭiyāṉ ciṟṟampalattāṉ” ñāṉa. 61.9) eṉavum, “poykaiyiṉ poḻiluṟu putumalart teṉṟalār, vaiyaiyiṉ vaṭakarai maruviya ēṭakam” (ñāṉa. 290.6) eṉavum ciṟappittaliṉ iv vēṭakat tēṉūrē caṅkakālat tēṉūr eṉalām.

**	“aṉṉa meṉṉaṭai naṉṉīrp poykai, āmpal nāṟum tēm poti naṟuvirait, tāmaraic cevvāyt taṇṇaṟaṟ kūntal” eṉavarum itaṟku, arumpatavuraikārar, “aṉṉamākiya meṉṉaṭaiyiṉaiyum, āmpal nāṟum naṟuviraiyaiyum, tāmarai
yākiya cevvāyaiyum, aṟalākiya kūntalaiyu muṭaiya poykaiyākiya peṇ” eṉṟār. eṉavē, itaṉkaṇ kūṟiya naṭai, virai, vāy, kūntal eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṟku muṟaiyē aṉṉamum, āmpalum, tāmaraiyum, aṟalum uvamaikaḷāka niṉṟaṉavām. iṉi aṭiyārkku nallār, tēmpoti naṟuvirai āmpal nāṟum tāmaraiyeṉa iyaittup poruḷkoḷvar; “iṉit tēmpoti naṟuvirait tāmaraiyākiya 
āmpal nāṟum cevvāy eṉiṉumamaiyum” eṉa urai vikaṟpaḻum kūṟuvar. eṉiṉum, ivvaiṅkuṟuṉūnūṟṟup pāṭṭiṉ karuttai nōkkiṉ, arumpatavuraikārar 
kūṟuvatē uṇmaiyuraiyātal viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iḵtu aṟiñar ārāyccik kuriya
toṉṟu.
